.animate li:nth-of-type(4) {
    animation: bounce4 0.5s 0.4s alternate backwards;
}

    animation: bounce5 0.5s 0.5s alternate backwards;
}

.animate li:nth-of-type(6) {
    animation: bounce6 0.5s 0.6s alternate backwards;
}

        opacity: 0 !important;
